# What stands at a bulge that is not 1 — the renders, trimmed
# Made by m10_10_evidence_interval (CAD_M10_10_DOC=<name>, CAD_M10_10_RULES=shipped|off,
# CAD_M10_10_EXPLAIN=6) at eps = 1e-9, dev build, on the tree of the fix pass. Forms are
# cut at 420 characters except in the last section, which carries the boss's freeze cause
# uncut; coefficient mantissas are as rendered (m·2^e). `?#id` is a node the walk froze (an
# opaque indeterminate); FROZEN in an explain tree is the node the ring or the budget refused.
# Only the 1e-9 renders are committed: the eps independence of the split (the same table and
# the same atom census at 1e-6 and 1e-12) is held by the pins in m10_bulge_interval, which
# the matrix runs at all three rows.
